Author: Ian Anthony
Date: 13 hours ago
Rating: 1 star
​This surgery provides an appalling standard of care that feels entirely devoid of empathy. While the receptionists and a few select GPs are professional, the overarching culture is dismissive and treats patients with blatant contempt. My experience included clinical reports that inaccurately represented consultations and a total disregard for confirmed symptoms. It is a sterile, 'conveyor-belt' system where the priority is clearly processing numbers and prescribing medication over genuine patient welfare. A disturbing example of a broken local service that lacks basic integrity and patient centred care...

Author: keekz Dannielle

Rating: 5 stars
Where do I begin 😭 I think doctor was Mr Yacoob anyway my Five week old was screaming for weeks I just put it down to being new he was red while drinking his milk, and he had a purple tint to his skin all the time his skin was rashy and red but he had been like that since he was born pretty much so I thought it was just him.. anyway I had about 8 hours sleep in two weeks tbf hardly slept in weeks couldn’t put him down I put it down to him being clingy I was sleep deprived baby just wasn’t happy,so i started to think something is not right, I took him to the doctors and he switched his milk and already the difference in my son I could cry with happiness he’s got a beautiful colour to his now instantly the rash and dry skin is already disappearing he’s loving his feeds no tears I’ve been able to put him down without a melt down and he is like a different baby already played with his toy for the first time today and me and my boy have actually slept and even had naps together could not thank the Dr enough I hope he sees this review thank you from me and Ivar ✨

Author: Chika Munky
Date: 8 years ago
Rating: 5 stars
I know that the reception staff get a pretty bad rep (I've experienced my share of bad experiences in the past). I thought I'd share the positive experience I had on Monday (04/09/17). The receptionist I spoke to on the phone was very helpful in booking my son in for an emergency appointment the same day, after discovering he was suffering from a uti. I was seen to very quickly and was told he would need to leave a sample. Since I had my baby in arm, I asked if the reception staff would mind holding her while I helped my son, who were more than happy to oblige. When I returned everyone was happy and laughing, including my 10 month daughter. Thank you very much for the positive experience.
